function initFreshChat() {
        window.fcWidget.init({
          token: 'd5109f5f-3b45-451b-ad72-5ad797321ce7',
          host: 'https://tfg.freshchat.com',
        })
        // To set user info
        window.fcWidget.user.setFirstName('Tshepang')
        window.fcWidget.user.setEmail("matsheps20@gmail.com");
      }

      function loadFreshChatScript(document, scriptId) {
        if (document.getElementById(scriptId)) {
          initFreshChat()
        } else {
          var script = document.createElement('script')
          script.id = scriptId
          script.async = true
          script.src = 'https://tfg.freshchat.com/js/widget.js'
          script.onload = initFreshChat
          document.head.appendChild(script)
        }
      }

      function initiateCall() {
        loadFreshChatScript(document, 'FreshChat')
      }

      if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initiateCall)
      } else {
        initiateCall()
      }
